2026-10-24T16:50:22.2349327Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2349892Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2350308Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2350769Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2351390Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2352115Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2352397Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2353040Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2353609Z         """
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2353999Z         try:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2354461Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2355045Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2355527Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2356045Z             return True
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2356350Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2356657Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2357071Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2357388Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2357901Z         return bool(match)

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2360333Z         """

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2362412Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2362826Z             with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2363323Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2363708Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2363957Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2364547Z             msg = "Cannot write language configuration file: {}".format(e.strerror)

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2569458Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2570060Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2573104Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2573966Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2574189Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2574740Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2575347Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2576058Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2576625Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2577272Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2577794Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2578166Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2578582Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2578976Z             #
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2579236Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2579727Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2580248Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2580753Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2582007Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2582419Z             #
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2582824Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2583214Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2583403Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2583927Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2584427Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2584873Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2585148Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2585294Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2585657Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2585985Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2586324Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2586734Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2587118Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2587473Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2587875Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2588330Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2588859Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2589349Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2589776Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2590267Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2590797Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2591165Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2591320Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2591754Z

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2604980Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2605452Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2605685Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2605892Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2606290Z         """

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2608782Z                 options,
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2609040Z             )
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2609181Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2609484Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2610183Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2610689Z         # decide.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2610916Z         log.debug(
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2611369Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2612039Z             options,
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2612287Z         )
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2612631Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2613134Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2613258Z         # store configuration from user
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2613657Z         super().set_layouts(layouts_variants, options, convert)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2613953Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2614085Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2614495Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2614773Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2615034Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2615618Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2615928Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2616158Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2616608Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2616899Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2617330Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2617683Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2617924Z         """
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2618246Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2618681Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2618916Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2619014Z         try:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2619322Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2619732Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2620039Z             return True
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2620292Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2620673Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2622366Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2622731Z             return False
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2622896Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2622995Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2623279Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2623766Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2624132Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2624385Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2624864Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2625044Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2625196Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2625644Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2625906Z         The result should be:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2626176Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2626342Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2626513Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2626802Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2627030Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2627452Z         """
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2627725Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2628249Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2628732Z         return new_list
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2628894Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2629011Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2629367Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2629639Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2629930Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2630664Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2631346Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2632077Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2632334Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2632544Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2632960Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2633482Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2633840Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2634070Z         """
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2634326Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2634656Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2634798Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2634916Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2635405Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2635886Z             return False
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2636048Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2636148Z         # find next layout
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2636467Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2636833Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2637135Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2637469Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2637729Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2637822Z         try:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2638152Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2638643Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2639058Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2639378Z             return True
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2639649Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2640080Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2640566Z             return False

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734005Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734260Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734409Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734413Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734525Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734678Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734767Z        -> return these
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2734953Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2735056Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2735200Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2735407Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2735616Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2735749Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2735754Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2735969Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2736104Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2736260Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2736391Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2736653Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2736754Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2736920Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2745178Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2745309Z     """
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2745430Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2745738Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2745857Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2745864Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2746260Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2746553Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2746669Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2746895Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2747063Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2747068Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2747184Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2747481Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2747591Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2747601Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2747719Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2748036Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2748807Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2748942Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2748948Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2748952Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2749209Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2749320Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2749500Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2749784Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2749897Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2750000Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2750179Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2750447Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2750716Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2750721Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2750831Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2756554Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2756807Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2757057Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2757292Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2757392Z #
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2757484Z # Usage:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2757736Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2757978Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758164Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758414Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758502Z #
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758603Z set -eu
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758608Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758710Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758804Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2758921Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759011Z do
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759116Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759213Z         in
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759319Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759441Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759721Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759819Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2759912Z     esac
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760001Z done
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760005Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760187Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760195Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760302Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760414Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760509Z then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760614Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760711Z fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760715Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2760918Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2761044Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2761191Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2761304Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2761838Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2761992Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2762087Z     #
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2762336Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2762456Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2762641Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2762781Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2762876Z     else
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2763134Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2763461Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2763466Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2763681Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2763808Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2763929Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764031Z       fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764121Z     fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764125Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764271Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764466Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764477Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764776Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2764899Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765125Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765243Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765372Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765466Z     else
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765635Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765725Z     fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765730Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2765920Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2766033Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2766127Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2766131Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2766242Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2766611Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2766869Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2766970Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767064Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767068Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767223Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767351Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767589Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767688Z             exit 1
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767778Z         fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2767875Z         sleep 1
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2768002Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2768098Z     done
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2768102Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2768427Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2768869Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2768995Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2769217Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2769527Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2769640Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2769731Z     else
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2769957Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770070Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770170Z     fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770263Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770268Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770373Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770478Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770483Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770743Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2770922Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771115Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771119Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771231Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771319Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771378Z     then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771842Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771928Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2771988Z     fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772133Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772136Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772274Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772394Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772494Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772498Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772659Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772662Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2772743Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773137Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773143Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773465Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773469Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773548Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773613Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773616Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773619Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773691Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773755Z then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773826Z     /bin/sleep infinity &
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773899Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2773968Z else
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774050Z     USER_INFO=$(setup_user_environment)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774159Z     INSTALLER_USER=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f1)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774248Z     HOME_DIR=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f2)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774252Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774367Z     # if we have netcat, use it for waiting until ws is up
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774451Z     if type nc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774521Z         for _ in `seq 10`; do
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774607Z             nc -z "$WEBUI_ADDRESS" 80 && break
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774670Z             sleep 0.5;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774734Z         done
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774800Z     else
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774870Z         # otherwise, just wait a bit
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774936Z         sleep 3
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774993Z     fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2774996Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2775317Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2775423Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2775516Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD=$(get_default_browser)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2775630Z     echo "Using default browser: ${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD}" >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2775731Z     if [[ "${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD}" == "firefox" ]]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2775828Z         eval "$(prepare_firefox_cmd "$INSTALLER_USER")"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2775948Z         echo "Using Firefox browser command: ${BROWSER[@]}" >&2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2776016Z     else

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778019Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778111Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778239Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778316Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778378Z fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778381Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778574Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778644Z strstr() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778713Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778777Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778779Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778858Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778918Z action() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778985Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2778988Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779049Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779135Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779199Z     shift
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779261Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779338Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779402Z     else
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779478Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779539Z     fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779597Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779666Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779730Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779794Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779797Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2779928Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780005Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780141Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780205Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780288Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780353Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780436Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780518Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780643Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780728Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2780923Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2781001Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2781131Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2781206Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2781310Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2781390Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2781394Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2781615Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2785698Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2785862Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2785867Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2785974Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786073Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786179Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786283Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786377Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786488Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786595Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786661Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786732Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786794Z         ;;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786857Z esac
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786860Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2786937Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787005Z success() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787096Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787156Z     return 0
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787218Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787222Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787291Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787358Z failure() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787424Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787505Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787604Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787666Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787729Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787732Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787802Z echo_success() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787879Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2787950Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788040Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788121Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788339Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788405Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788473Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788532Z     return 0
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788595Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788598Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788665Z echo_failure() {

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788805Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2788961Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789044Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789106Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789175Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789234Z     return 1
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789297Z }
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789299Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789361Z fscheck() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789431Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789498Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789563Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789642Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789707Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789782Z         ext[432])
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789853Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2789936Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790000Z             ;;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790064Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790130Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790210Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790270Z             ;;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790334Z         btrfs)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790433Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790645Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790712Z             ;;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790773Z         xfs)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790854Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790930Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2790992Z             ;;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2791056Z         f2fs)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2791134Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2791211Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2791269Z             ;;
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2791333Z         *)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2791457Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2794287Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2794391Z     esac
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2794479Z }

2026-10-24T16:50:22.2814338Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2814581Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2814857Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2815125Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2815400Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2815636Z   fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2815779Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2816109Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2816378Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2816754Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2817006Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2817256Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2817581Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2817972Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2818209Z     fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2818490Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2818725Z   fi
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2819144Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2819312Z
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2819581Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2819973Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2820214Z     echo
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2820490Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2820902Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T16:50:22.2821162Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
